238 CUSTOMS GAZETTE. [JAN.-MAR., NOTICES TO MARINERS. CHINA SEA. No. 389. YANGTZE RIVER-CHINKIANG DISTRICT. SILVER ISLAND CROSSING AND TASHA CHANNEL. ALTERATIONS IN LIGHTS. NOTICE is hereby given that, owing to the silting up of the Silver Island Crossing, the Tasha (or Round) Channel has been marked by a light-boat, showing a red light. The boat is moored at the upper end of the crossipg off Chinm4m in 29 feet of water, and marks the starboard side of the channel. From the light-boat, Silver Island Crossing Low (or Outer) Range Beacon bears S. 82&#39; W., and is distant 2 miles. , / . t During low river, and until the Silver island Crossing deepens, the red light on the High (or Inner) Range Beacon will not be exhibitg. All bearings given are magnetic, and depths are reduced to zero of the Chinkiang river gauge. SHANGHAI, I ith January 1904. No. 390. YANGTZE RIVER-KIUKIANG DISTRICT. CHRISTMAS ISLAND CUT-OFF AND TAITZJICHI CHANNEL. ALTERATIONS IN LIGHTS. NOTICE is hereby given that the Christmas Island Cut-off Light-boat and the Hen Point Beacon Light have been discontinued. And that a light-boat, from which a red light is exhibited, has been placed off the spit at the mouth of the stream situated to the westward of Taitztichi, to mark the starboard side of the channel for vessels bound up river. SHANGHAI, I Ith January 1904.

NOTICE is hereby given that the Coker Rocks West Buoy, Amoy Inner Harbour, has been moved about i 5o feet west of its former position. SHANGHAI, i9th January 1904. No. 392. SHANGHAI DISTRICT. TSUNGMING CROSSING. ALTERATIONS IN BuoyAGE. NoTe is hereby given that, in consequence of changes having taken place in the channel across the Tsungming Banks, the East Entrance Gas-lighted Buoy has been altered to a Fairway Buoy, i.e., painted in red and blck horizontal bands, and that the First Crossing Buoy has been shifted 1.3 cables S. i&#39; E. N OTE. To -find the best water vessels should pass each buoy, tcco&#39;ding to its colour, at a distance of about i cable. .SHANGHAI, 24th Febmary 1904. No. 393. CHINKIANG DISTRICT. GALLOWS AND YIKSANG CHANNELS. ALTERATIONS IN LIGHTS. NOTICE is hereby given that, in consequence of the silting up of the Gallows Channel, the light-boat marking it has been discontinued, and that a new channel, known as the Yiksang Channel, having cut through between May Queen and Gros Islands, a light-boat, to be known as the May Queen Island Light-boat, has been placed off the north-eastern side of that island, and marks the port side of the channel for vessels bound up river. SHANGHAI, 24th February 104.
